doc_pred in predictions_by_doc:\\n        doc_id = doc_pred[\\\"doc_id\\\"]\\n        raw_doc = doc_map.get(doc_id)\\n        if raw_doc is None:\\n            continue\\n\\n        name_to_idx = triple.get(\\\"relation\\\", \\\"\\\").lower().strip()\\n            rel_code = REL_NAME_TO_CODE.get(rel_name)\\n            if rel_code is None:\\n                stats[\\\"skipped_rel\\\"] += 1\\n                continue\\n\\n    \\n\\n... parser.add_argument(\\\"--sft_adapter\\\", default=None)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--data_path\\\", required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output_dir\\\", required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--batch_size\\\", type=int, default=16)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--max_new_tokens\\\", type=int, default=1024)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--seed\\\", type=int, default=42)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--no_evidence\\\", action=\\\"store_true\\\", default=False)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--quantize\\\", action=argparse.BooleanOptionalAction, default=True)\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\\n    if torch.cuda.is_available():\\n        torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(args.seed)\\n\\n    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n\\n    adapter_config = Path(args.model_path) / \\\"adapter_config.json\\\"\\n    if adapter_config.exists():\\n        base_model_path = args.base_model\\n        adapter_path = args.model_path\\n        logger.info(\\\"Detected adapter at %s, base: %s\\\", adapter_path, base_model_path)\\n    else:\\n include_evi = not args.no_evidence\\n    eval_items, raw_docs = prepare_test_data(\\n        args.data_path, tokenizer,\\n        include_evidence=include_evi, system_prompt=sys_prompt,\\n    )\\n\\n    t0 = time.time()\\n    raw_results = run_inference(model, tokenizer, eval_items, args.batch_size, args.max_new_tokens)\\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\\n    logger.info(\\\"Inference: %.1fs (%.2f s/doc)\\\", elapsed, elapsed / max(len(eval_items), 1))\\n\\n    detailed = []\\n    for r in raw_results:\\n        detailed.append({\\n            \\\"doc_id\\\": r[\\\"doc_id\\\"],\\n r[\\\"format_ok\\\"],\\n        })\\n\\n    pred_path = output_dir / \\\"predictions.json\\\"\\n    with open(pred_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(detailed, f, indent=2, Now I have a complete picture. Let me also check `test_rsft_s44/predictions.json` - it seems the test eval failed with all zeros, which probably means the test_inference.py has a bug where it writes zeros when there's no gold labels (test set has no gold). Wait, looking again:

The `test_rsft_s44/metrics.json` shows all zeros: `n_documents: 0, n_predictions: 0, n_format_ok: 0, inference_time_seconds: 0.0`. This means the inference didn't actually run at all or was killed. But `config` shows `split: "test"` — wait, the config has `"split": "test"` but test_inference.py doesn't have a `--split` argument... Let me look more carefully.


Actually, I see now — the config has `"split": "test"` which means it was run using inference.py (not test_inference.py), and inference.py does support `--split test`. The all-zeros result makes sense because the DocRED test set has no gold labels (they're held out for CodaLab), so the evaluator returns zeros when it can't find any gold triples to evaluate against. That's why `plan_002_test_eval_s44` failed.

The right approach is to use test_inference.py instead, which generates predictions and a CodaLab submission file without trying to evaluate locally. For plan_002, I should run inference on the test set with test_inference.py to generate the submission, then the user submits it to CodaLab for actual evaluation.

Now I'm checking what the flat_nli_s43 experiment produced and looking at the SFT baseline eval results to understand the output format and metrics being used.

\"/root/autodl-tmp/eval_results/dpo_ced/metrics.json\"},\n]\n\n\ndef load_metrics(cfg):\n    path = Path(cfg[\"path\"])\n    if not path.exists():\n        return None\n    with open(path) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    if \"key\" in cfg:\n        data = data[cfg[\"key\"]]\n    return data\n\n\ndef get_val(m, *keys):\n    for k in keys:\n        if k in m:\n            return m[k]\n    return 0\n\n\ndef main():\n    results = []\n    for cfg in EVAL_CONFIGS:\n        m = load_metrics(cfg)\n        if m is None:\n            print(f\"MISSING: {cfg['name']} -> {cfg['path']}\")\n            continue\n        results.append((cfg[\"name\"], m))\n\n    if not results:\n        print(\"No results found!\")\n        return\n\n    sft_f1 = get_val(results[0][1], \"f1\", \"rel_f1\")\n    sft_edcr = get_val(results[0][1], \"edcr\")\n\n    header = (f\"{'Method':<28} {'rel_f1':>7} {'Δf1':>8} {'evi_f1':>7} \"\n              f\"{'EDCR':>7} {'ΔEDCR':>8} {'evi_j':>7} {'preds':>6} {'trunc':>6}\")\n    print(header)\n    print(\"-\" * len(header))\n\n    table_data = []\n    for name, m in results:\n        f1 = get_val(m, \"f1\", \"rel_f1\")\n        evi_f1 = get_val(m, \"evi_f1\")\n        edcr = get_val(m, \"edcr\")\n        evi_joint = get_val(m, \"evi_f1_joint\")\n        delta_f1 = (f1 - sft_f1) * 100\n        delta_edcr = (edcr - sft_edcr) * 100\n        preds = get_val(m, \"n_predictions\", \"pred_count\")\n        trunc = m.get(\"n_truncated\")\n        trunc_str = str(trunc) if trunc is not None else \"-\"\n\n        print(f\"{name:<28} {f1:>7.4f} {delta_f1:>+7.2f}p {evi_f1:>7.4f} \"\n              f\"{edcr:>7.4f} {delta_edcr:>+7.2f}p {evi_joint:>7.4f} {preds:>6} {trunc_str:>6}\")\n\n        table_data.append({\n            \"method\": name,\n            \"rel_f1\": round(f1, 4),\n            \"evi_f1\": round(evi_f1, 4),\n            \"edcr\": round(edcr, 4),\n            \"evi_f1_joint\": round(evi_joint, 4),\n            \"delta_rel_f1_pp\": round(delta_f1, 2),\n            \"delta_edcr_pp\": round(delta_edcr, 2),\n            \"precision\": round(get_val(m, \"precision\"), 4),\n            \"recall\": \"RSFT-CED (s43, 3ep)\", \"RSFT-CED (s44, 3ep)\"}\n    rsft_seeds = [(n, m) for n, m in results if n in seed_names]\n    if len(rsft_seeds) == 3:\n        print()\n        print(\"--- RSFT-CED 3-seed (s42/s43/s44) ---\")\n        for k, label in [(\"f1\", \"rel_f1\"), (\"evi_f1\", \"evi_f1\"), (\"edcr\", \"EDCR\"), (\"evi_f1_joint\", \"evi_j\")]:\n            vals = [get_val(m, k) for _, m in rsft_seeds]\n            if all(v > 0 for v in vals):\n                mean_v = statistics.mean(vals)\n                std_v = statistics.stdev(vals)\n                print(f\"  {label:>7} = {mean_v:.4f} ± {std_v:.4f}\")\n\n        table_data.append({\n            \"method\": \"RSFT-CED 3-seed mean\",\n            \"rel_f1\": round(statistics.mean([get_val(m, \"f1\") for _, m in rsft_seeds]), 4),\n            \"rel_f1_std\": round(statistics.stdev([get_val(m, \"f1\") for _, m in rsft_seeds]), 4),\n            \"evi_f1\": round(statistics.mean([get_val(m, \"evi_f1\") for _, m in rsft_seeds]), 4),\n            \"evi_f1_std\": round(statistics.stdev([get_val(m, \"evi_f1\") for _, m in rsft_seeds]), 4),\n            \"edcr\": round(statistics.mean([get_val(m, \"edcr\") for _, m in rsft_seeds]), 4),\n            \"edcr_std\": round(statistics.stdev([get_val(m, \"edcr\") for _, m in rsft_seeds]), 4),\n        })\n\n    output_path = Path(\"/root/autodl-tmp/eval_results/cross_method_table.json\")\n    with open(output_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(table_data, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n    print(f\"\\nExported -> The cross_method_table runs successfully. A few things to note:

1. DPO-CED metrics not found at the expected path — need to check where they are
2. Some `evi_f1_joint` values show 0 — this is because older eval runs didn't compute this metric, only the newer ones have it

The registry has evi_f1_joint values for the RSFT runs (around 0.34-0.37), but the table is showing 0 — looks like the older metrics.json files weren't updated with these computed values, so there's a mismatch between what's in the registry and what's actually in the stored metrics.
